                \texttt{Player F: Continuing F down C1: moving from R3C1 to R4C1, en route to target R6C1. Keeping to the left edge, no interference with Xs at R4C5/R8C5 or E at R8C7. I’ll need C1 clear for the next couple of moves.} \\
            }
        }
    }
    & & \\ \\

    \theutterance \stepcounter{utterance}
